For q,r in [0,m-1]. For k in [0,2C] (0..4m-2). Let d = k-C.

Potential algorithms in detail:

A. Anti-pal binary random sampling:
Function make_random_anti_binary(m, rng): n=2m. For each q in 0..m-1, choose left boolean. Create zeros length n. If left: arr[q]=1; else arr[n-1-q]=1. Use rng.random(m)<0.5. Return arr.
Score via FFT: _fft_square(arr) returns c. score = 2*n*c.max()/arr.sum()**2. arr.sum()=m.
Batch sampling: To reduce overhead, could generate several at once. For m=8192, matrix of batch B x m bits -> B x 2m floats memory: B=64, 64*16384*8=8MB, okay.   left = rng.random((batch, m)) <0.5
  arr = zeros((batch, 2m))
  indices q = arange(m)
  arr[:, left?] Hard to assign 2D conditional. We can do:
    arr[:, left? no 2d].
  Use np.put? Simpler:
    arr = np.zeros((batch,2m))
    # left positions
    left_idx = np.where(left) gives B*m maybe large but okay for B=64 (1M). Could be slow.
  Alternative construct using two indices:
    for b in range(batch): one-python loop, okay if batch small and total candidates maybe 10000 -> 10000 loops; each array allocation 16k; maybe 0.2s? Actually 10000*16k fill=160M, okay. But FFT per sequence maybe slower.
  Could generate one by one in loop. Simpler and memory safe. Use rng.integers(0,2,m). For each, zeros and set. Python overhead per candidate maybe few ms due allocation; 10k=5s. FFT 10k*0.002=20s. Fine.
  To batch, we can do:

    arr = np.zeros((B,2*m), dtype=np.float64)
    # Set left ones: arr[:, left] can't because left 2D boolean indexing yields 1D positions over whole array: arr[left] = 1 works. Actually arr[left]=1 sets left positions; arr[~left]=1 sets all other positions including symmetric? We need only for each pair exactly one. But boolean indexing with positions:

B. Local sign search on best binary:
Given z of length m (left=+1 else -1) for n=2m, t=1/m (or binary values). Flipping sign q changes left/right. We can maintain arr and c. For each candidate flip q, compute new max quickly? We can update c by difference. We can implement a greedy "first improvement" sweep:
  For q from 0 to m-1 (or random order), evaluate if flip improves objective (e.g., smooth logsumexp or max with penalty). If improves, update arr/c.
But O(m*n) per sweep if O(n) update per q. For n=8192, m*n=33M; if done in Python loops over q and vectorized update length n, 4096*8192 ~33M float ops per sweep in C if vectorized? But allocating delta each time expensive. We can precompute shift arrays? For each q, cross term = 2*t*(P shifted r - P shifted q). To compute new c = c + 2t(P_shift_r - P_shift_q) + t^2( -2 δ_C + δ_{2q}+δ_{2r})? Since flipping z: old V = t/2 z (x^q - x^r). New V' = -V? If z flips, V -> -V. Then P' = U - V. Change = P' - P = -2V. Let's compute continuous model for random anti-pal. Let q,r independent uniform [0,m] (scale m=1, C=2). Choices independent ±. Sum S = X+Y where X=q or 2-q. The density? Cases:
1: LL: q+r, q,r∈[0,1] -> support [0,2], density triangular, probability 1/4.
2: RR: (2-q)+(2-r)=4-(q+r), support [2,4], density triangular prob1/4.
3: LR: q + (2-r)=2 + (q-r), support [1,3], difference distribution triangular prob1/4.
4: RL: same support [1,3] prob1/4.

_LBFGS_CACHE = {}
def _obj_x(x, args):
    tau, n = args
    res = _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau)
    _LBFGS_CACHE['x'] = x.copy()
    _LBFGS_CACHE['grad'] = res[1]
    _LBFGS_CACHE['tau'] = tau
    return res[0]
def _jac_x(x, args):
    tau,n=args
    if 'grad' in _LBFGS_CACHE and np.array_equal(_LBFGS_CACHE['x'], x) and _LBFGS_CACHE['tau']==tau:
         return _LBFGS_CACHE['grad']
    return _eval_x_grad(x,n,tau)[1]

Let's define _optimize_full_lbfgs(a0, alloc, rng, label):

Potential numerical issue: L-BFGS on z has reparameterization invariance (adding constant to z no change). This direction has zero gradient; L-BFGS may waste. We can remove mean? def _obj_x(x, args):
    tau, n = args
    val, gx = _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau)
    _CACHE_X['x'] = x
    _CACHE_X['tau'] = tau
    _CACHE_X['grad'] = gx
    return val
def _jac_x(x, args):
    tau,n=args
    cx=_CACHE_X
    if (cx.get('tau')==tau and 'x' in cx and 'grad' in cx and np.shape(cx['x'])==np.shape(x) and np.array_equal(cx['x'], x)):
         return cx['grad']
    return _eval_x_grad(x,n,tau)[1]

Function _softmax_weights(c,tau):

Given f (a), M=max c. Solve approximately LP: max ∑g s.t. a*g≤M, g≥0. The dual: min M∑y s.t. (a*y)_i ≥1, y≥0. We can solve dual via multiplicative weights: update y_k ← y_k * exp(η(1-(a*y)_k?)) Wait constraint (a*y)_i≥1. This is a vector of length n, y length L. Objective ∑y. We want nonnegative y that cover constraints with a*y. This is max? Multiplicative weights for covering: Start y uniform. At each iter, compute g_i=(a*y)_i. Sinster constraints with low g; update y to increase where? The dual variables y on convolution coefficients. The primal variables g_i positive where g_i? KKT. But maybe overkill.

If we get y, the LP primal directions g active where y? The state-of-art g maybe solution of max sum subject to a*g≤M. The dual y gives support of g? Complementary slackness: g_i>0 => (a*y)_i=1. So we could choose g_i=1 for active set and solve? But need maximize sum; active set determined by y. We can simply set g_i=1 where (a*y)_i close to min (1) and g=0 else, then normalize? This is like state-of-art extreme point. This might produce a good g with many zeros. Then update a toward g. This is interesting and cheap to approximate.

Algorithm (state-of-art simplified):
- Given a normalized sum1, M=max(a*a).
- Solve/iterate dual y: need a*y ≈1 (componentwise lower bound). We can find y by deconvolution: if a positive, y ≈ 1/a in frequency sense. But y must nonnegative. Use iterative: y ← y * exp(η * (1 - a*y)/(max)) projected nonnegative. For a few iterations.
- Determine active_i = (a*y)_i. The LP optimum g supported on active constraints with value 1 (tight). We can set g_i = 1 where active_i within tolerance of min (which should be 1), maybe also top? Then normalize g to sum1. Update a←(1-t)a + t g, project nonnegative. Repeat.
This is heuristic. The previous code used weights p based on a*a peaks; state-of-art LP might find g with sparsity. Could be effective. But uncertain.

# eval x grad
def _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau):
    m=n//2; eps=1e-7
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x=np.clip(x, eps, 1.0-eps)
    t=1.0/m
    a=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    idx=np.arange(m)
    a[idx]=t*x
    a[n-1-idx]=t*(1.0-x)
    c=_fft_square(a)
    if c.size==0: return 0.0, np.zeros(m)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 1e-300: return 0.0, np.zeros(m)
    p, val = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
    h=_fft_grad(p,a)
    ga=2.0*h
    gx=t*(ga[idx] - ga[n-1-idx])
    gx=np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), gx

def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    L=len(c)
    if cmax <=1e-300:
       p=np.full(L,1.0/L); return p,0.0
    if tau <=1e-12*cmax:
       p=np.zeros(L); mask = c>=cmax-1e-14
       if not mask.any(): p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
       else: p[mask]=1.0/mask.sum()
       return p, cmax
    vals=(c-cmax)/tau
    vals=np.clip(vals, -745.0, 0.0)
    ex=np.exp(vals)
    s=float(ex.sum())
    if s <=1e-300:
       p=np.zeros(L); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else:
       p=ex/s
    val=cmax + tau*np.log(s)
    return p,float(val)

Note tau <=1e-12*cmax threshold: If tau very small but cmax maybe, use onehot.

def _optimize_fourier(n, K, amp, restarts, maxiter, rng, label=''):
   m=n//2
   basis=_make_fourier_basis(m,K)
   best_x=None; best_cmax=inf

      # initial tau
      x=_x_from_fourier(theta,basis,amp)
      a=_a_from_x(x,n); c=_fft_square(a); cmax=c.max(); tau0=cmax/5.0 if cmax>0 else 1e-6

         # optional nudge with rng if no progress
   return best_x,best_cmax

```
def _optimize_full_expg(a0, time_limit, rng, focus_start=5.0, max_focus=1000.0):
    a=np.asarray(a0).ravel().astype(np.float64)
    n=a.size
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: a=np.ones(n)/n
    else: a=s/a? Actually a/=s
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=a.sum()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_internal_score(a)
    b=None; cmax=inf; no_improve=0; max_no=800; resets=0; max_resets=25
    focus=focus_start; eta=0.5; eta_min=0.005; eta_max=10; t0=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if b is None:
          b=_fft_square(a); cmax=float(b.max()); score=2*n*cmax
       if cmax*2*n < best_score -1e-12: best... no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > max_no: reset (mix random anti or multiply)
       p,_=_weights_focus(b,focus)
       g=2*_fft_grad(p,a); gd=g-np.dot(a,g)
       maxg=...; if < eps break
       gd = gd/maxg
       a_try = a*np.exp(-eta*gd); a_try=np.maximum(try,1e-15); a_try/=sum
       b_try=_fft_square(a_try); cmax_try=b_try.max(); score_try=2*n*cmax_try
       # accept if improves cmax or weighted
       wt_old=np.dot(p,b); wt_new=np.dot(p,b_try)
       accept=False
       if score_try < score -1e-12: accept=True
       elif cmax_try < cmax*1.001 and wt_new < wt_old -1e-14: accept=True
       # also if no_improve high, accept slight improvements
       if accept: a=a_try; b=b_try; cmax=cmax_try; eta=min...
       else: eta=max; if eta<0.01: perturb
       focus=min(focus*1.01,max_focus)
    return best_a,best_score
```

```
def _internal_score(a, b=None):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  if a.size==0: return inf
  b_compute needed. s=float(a.sum())
  if s<0.01: return inf
  if b is None: b=_fft_square(a)
  maxb=float(b.max())
  return float(2.0*a.size*maxb/(s*s))
```

The O(n) update method is attractive but careful with float drift. We can include a short polish for best_x:
```
def _polish_x(a,x,n, time_limit, rng):
  # only if n even
  P=a.copy(); x=x.copy(); c=_fft_square(P); best_max=c.max(); best_a=P.copy(); best_x=x.copy()
  t=1.0/(n//2)
  t0=time.monotonic()
  no_improve=0
  while time < time_limit and no_improve < 5:
    order=rng.permutation(m)
    improved=False
    for i in order:
      l=i; r=n-1-i; old_left=P[l]; old_right=P[r]
      # try target values 0,1, maybe 0.5
      for target in (0.0,1.0,0.5):
         new_left=t*target; delta=new_left-old_left
         if abs(delta)<1e-12: continue
         if new_left < -1e-12 or new_left > t+1e-12: continue
         # apply update
         c[l:l+n] += 2.0*delta*P
         c[r:r+n] -= 2.0*delta*P
         c[2*l] += delta*delta
         c[2*r] += delta*delta
         c[l+r] -= 2.0*delta*delta
         new_max = float(c.max())
         if new_max < best_max - 1e-14:
            # commit: set P and x? To avoid drift, recompute c after update
            P[l]=new_left; P[r]=t-new_left; x[i]=target; c=_fft_square(P); best_max=c.max(); best_a=P.copy(); best_x=x.copy(); improved=True
            break
         # revert
         c[r:r+n] += 2.0*delta*P
         c[l:l+n] -= 2.0*delta*P
         c[2*l] -= delta*delta
         c[2*r] -= delta*delta
         c[l+r] += 2.0*delta*delta
      # if best_max not improving global? 
    if not improved: no_improve +=1; maybe perturb
    # optionally random perturb if plateau: choose random i target uniform, if improves accept?
  return best_x,best_a, 2*n*best_max
```

Let's write _polish_x carefully:
```
def _polish_x(x0, n, time_limit, rng):
  m=n//2; t=1.0/m; eps=1e-12
  x=np.clip(np.asarray(x0), eps, 1-eps)
  P=_a_from_x(x,n)
  c=_fft_square(P)
  best_max=float(c.max()); best_P=P.copy(); best_x=x.copy()
  best_score=2*n*best_max
  t0=time.monotonic()
  no_sweeps=0
  trial_count=0
  while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
     if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
     improved=False
     order=rng.permutation(m)
     for i in order:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       l=i; r=n-1-i
       old_x=x[i]
       # candidates
       cands = (0.0, 1.0, 0.5)
       # also if old_x not near, maybe add? Use tuple dynamic? Cannot use list? okay list.
       # We'll just use for target in cands.
       for target in cands:
          if abs(target - old_x) < 1e-9: continue
          new_left = t*float(target)
          delta = new_left - P[l]
          if abs(delta) < 1e-12: continue
          if new_left < -1e-9 or new_left > t+1e-9: continue
          # trial
          c[l:l+n] += 2.0*delta*P
          c[r:r+n] -= 2.0*delta*P
          c[2*l] += delta*delta
          c[2*r] += delta*delta
          c[l+r] -= 2.0*delta*delta
          trial_count +=1
          if trial_count % 2000 ==0:
             # recompute to avoid drift? But if we recompute during trial, it overwrites trial values. We need revert first. Recompute after revert more suitable.
          new_max=float(c.max())
          if new_max < best_max - 1e-14:
             # commit
             P[l]=new_left; P[r]=t-new_left; x[i]=float(target)
             c=_fft_square(P)
             best_max=float(c.max()); best_P=P.copy(); best_x=x.copy(); best_score=2*n*best_max; improved=True
             break
          # revert
          c[r:r+n] += 2.0*delta*P
          c[l:l+n] -= 2.0*delta*P
          c[2*l] -= delta*delta
          c[2*r] -= delta*delta
          c[l+r] += 2.0*delta*delta
          trial_count +=1
          if trial_count % 5000 ==0:
             c=_fft_square(P)
             # best_max might be lower due previous commits? We recompute; if cmax > best_max? best_max is min seen; after revert should equal current max. Use current c.max maybe.
             if c.max() > best_max:
                 # drift caused current max > recorded; update current? best_max remains lower. But current trials compare to best_max; okay.
                 pass
     if not improved:
        no_sweeps +=1
        # random perturb: try 20 random moves to maybe escape
        rand_tried=0
        while no_sweeps < 3 and rand_tried < 50 and time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
           i=int(rng.integers(0,m)); target=float(rng.random())
           # evaluate like above; if improves commit
        if no_sweeps >=3: break
     else:
        no_sweeps=0
  return best_x, best_P, float(best_score)
```

But recompute during loop after 5000 trials: We should only recompute between moves, not during trial. In above, after revert. Good.

Function _weights_focus:
```
def _weights_focus(b, focus, eps=0.02):
  cmax=float(b.max()); L=b.size
  if cmax <=1e-300: return np.full(L,1.0/L), cmax
  z=(cmax-b)/cmax
  if focus > 0: p=np.exp(-focus*z)
  else: p=np.ones(L)
  s=p.sum()
  if s <=1e-12: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
  else: p=((1-eps)*p/s)+eps/L
  return p,cmax
```
This focuses high b. For exponent optimizer, focus increases; eta adaptive.

Potential issue: For a sequence with cmax small, focus large, p may underflow; exp(-focus*z) with z≥0, max at b=cmax ->1, min exp(-focus). If focus=1000, underflow to 0 for many, okay. p may become onehot. Good.

Now, full exponent update `gd = g - dot(a,g)` enforces sum-preserving exponential? Actually after exp update and renormalize, the projection is approximate. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)**2.

Strategy:
- Use a strong anti-palindromic ansatz a_i + a_{n-1-i} = constant.
- Optimize a smooth surrogate (split log-sum-exp) of the maximum convolution
  coefficient with L-BFGS in both the full anti-palindromic parameter and a
  low-dimensional Fourier parameterization.
- Also use a general log-sum-exp projected exponentiated-gradient optimizer
  on the full vector as a robust fallback/refinement.
- Polish the best anti-palindromic sequence with an exact-coordinate search
  that updates the full convolution in O(n) per trial.

The returned sequence is normalized and has non-negative float entries.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

# Global caches for objective/gradient pairs used by scipy.
_CACHE_X = {}
_CACHE_Z = {}
_CACHE_F = {}


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_square(a):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    """
    For c_k = sum_{i+j=k} a_i a_j, compute
      h_i = sum_k weights[k] * a_{k-i}, i=0..n-1.
    Then gradient of sum weights[k] c_k is 2*h.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(weights.size)
    N = _pow2(L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(weights, n=N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N)[:n]
    return h


def _a_from_x(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(x.size)
    t = 1.0 / max(1, m)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(m)
    a[idx] = t * x
    a[n - 1 - idx] = t * (1.0 - x)
    return a


def _internal_score(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if b is None:
        b = _fft_square(a)
    mb = float(b.max())
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mb / (s * s))


def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cmax = float(c.max())
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0 or cmax <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)
        return p, 0.0

    # Very small temperature: put weight on all maximizers.
    if tau <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax):
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        mask = c >= cmax - 1e-14
        if not np.any(mask):
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            p[mask] = 1.0 / float(mask.sum())
        return p, float(cmax)

    vals = (c - cmax) / tau
    vals = np.clip(vals, -745.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(vals)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = ex / s
    val = cmax + tau * float(np.log(s))
    return p, float(val)


def _random_positive(n, rng, amp=3.0):
    n = int(n)
    z = rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=n)
    z -= z.max()
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a /= a.sum()
    return a


def _x_from_sequence(a, n):
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return np.full(n // 2, 0.5, dtype=np.float64) if n > 1 else np.zeros(1)
    m = n // 2
    idx = np.arange(m)
    left = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)[idx]
    right = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)[n - 1 - idx]
    den = left + right
    x = np.full(m, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = den > 1e-12
    x[mask] = left[mask] / den[mask]
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _make_fourier_basis(m, K):
    m = int(m)
    K = int(K)
    if m <= 0 or K <= 0:
        return []
    s = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
    basis = []
    for k in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * s
        basis.append((np.sin(ang), np.cos(ang)))
    return basis


def _x_from_fourier(theta, basis, amp):
    if len(basis) == 0:
        return np.full(1, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    m = basis[0][0].size
    raw = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, (sn, cs) in enumerate(basis):
        raw += theta[2 * k] * sn + theta[2 * k + 1] * cs
    x = 0.5 + 0.5 * float(amp) * np.tanh(raw)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(x.size)
    n = int(n)
    eps = 1e-8
    x = np.clip(x, eps, 1.0 - eps)
    t = 1.0 / max(1, m)

    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(m)
    a[idx] = t * x
    a[n - 1 - idx] = t * (1.0 - x)

    c = _fft_square(a)
    p, val = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6

    h = _fft_grad(p, a)
    ga = 2.0 * h
    gx = t * (ga[idx] - ga[n - 1 - idx])
    gx = np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), gx


def _obj_x(x, args):
    tau, n = args
    val, gx = _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau)
    _CACHE_X["x"] = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _CACHE_X["tau"] = float(tau)
    _CACHE_X["grad"] = gx
    return float(val)


def _jac_x(x, args):
    tau, n = args
    if _CACHE_X.get("tau") == float(tau) and "x" in _CACHE_X and "grad" in _CACHE_X:
        cx = _CACHE_X["x"]
        if cx.shape == np.asarray(x).shape and np.array_equal(cx, np.asarray(x)):
            return _CACHE_X["grad"]
    return _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau)[1]


def _eval_z_grad(z, n, tau):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))

    zm = float(np.max(z))
    a = np.exp(z - zm)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    c = _fft_square(a)
    p, val = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6

    h = _fft_grad(p, a)
    ga = 2.0 * h
    mean_g = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gz = a * (ga - mean_g)
    gz = np.nan_to_num(gz, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), gz


def _obj_z(z, args):
    tau, n = args
    val, gz = _eval_z_grad(z, n, tau)
    _CACHE_Z["z"] = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _CACHE_Z["tau"] = float(tau)
    _CACHE_Z["grad"] = gz
    return float(val)


def _jac_z(z, args):
    tau, n = args
    if _CACHE_Z.get("tau") == float(tau) and "z" in _CACHE_Z and "grad" in _CACHE_Z:
        cz = _CACHE_Z["z"]
        if cz.shape == np.asarray(z).shape and np.array_equal(cz, np.asarray(z)):
            return _CACHE_Z["grad"]
    return _eval_z_grad(z, n, tau)[1]


def _eval_f_grad(theta, basis, m, n, tau, amp):
    x = _x_from_fourier(theta, basis, amp)
    val, gx = _eval_x_grad(x, n, tau)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e6

    raw = np.zeros(int(m), dtype=np.float64)
    for k, (sn, cs) in enumerate(basis):
        raw += theta[2 * k] * sn + theta[2 * k + 1] * cs
    th = np.tanh(raw)
    wgx = gx * (0.5 * float(amp) * (1.0 - th * th))

    g = np.zeros(2 * len(basis), dtype=np.float64)
    for k, (sn, cs) in enumerate(basis):
        g[2 * k] = float(np.dot(sn, wgx))
        g[2 * k + 1] = float(np.dot(cs, wgx))
    g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), g


def _obj_f(theta, args):
    basis, m, n, tau, amp = args
    val, g = _eval_f_grad(theta, basis, m, n, tau, amp)
    _CACHE_F["theta"] = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    _CACHE_F["tau"] = float(tau)
    _CACHE_F["n"] = int(n)
    _CACHE_F["grad"] = g
    return float(val)


def _jac_f(theta, args):
    basis, m, n, tau, amp = args
    if (
        _CACHE_F.get("tau") == float(tau)
        and _CACHE_F.get("n") == int(n)
        and "theta" in _CACHE_F
        and "grad" in _CACHE_F
    ):
        ct = _CACHE_F["theta"]
        if ct.shape == np.asarray(theta).shape and np.array_equal(ct, np.asarray(theta)):
            return _CACHE_F["grad"]
    return _eval_f_grad(theta, basis, m, n, tau, amp)[1]


def _optimize_fourier(n, K, amp, restarts, maxiter, rng):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 8:
        return None, float("inf")
    m = n // 2
    basis = _make_fourier_basis(m, K)
    if len(basis) == 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    best_x = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for _ in range(max(1, int(restarts))):
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=2 * len(basis))
        x = _x_from_fourier(theta, basis, amp)
        a = _a_from_x(x, n)
        c = _fft_square(a)
        cmax = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            continue
        tau0 = cmax / 5.0
        phases = [tau0, tau0 / 4.0, tau0 / 16.0, tau0 / 64.0]
        for tau in phases:
            _CACHE_F.clear()
            args = (basis, m, n, float(tau), float(amp))
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    _obj_f,
                    theta,
                    jac=_jac_f,
                    args=args,
                    method="BFGS",
                    options={"maxiter": max(int(maxiter), 10), "gtol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
                )
                if res.x is not None:
                    theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                pass

            x = _x_from_fourier(theta, basis, amp)
            a = _a_from_x(x, n)
            sc = _internal_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_x = x.copy()

    if best_x is None:
        x = _x_from_fourier(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=2 * len(basis)), basis, amp)
        a = _a_from_x(x, n)
        best_score = _internal_score(a)
        best_x = x.copy()
    return best_x, float(best_score)


def _optimize_pair_lbfgs(x0, n, rng, label=""):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = _a_from_x(x, n)
    c = _fft_square(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = _internal_score(a)
    cmax0 = float(c.max())
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY or not np.isfinite(cmax0) or cmax0 <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_x, float(best_score)

    tau_base = cmax0
    if n <= 8192:
        phases = [
            (tau_base / 5.0, 40),
            (tau_base / 20.0, 30),
            (tau_base / 80.0, 25),
            (tau_base / 320.0, 20),
        ]
    else:
        phases = [
            (tau_base / 5.0, 20),
            (tau_base / 20.0, 15),
            (tau_base / 80.0, 10),
        ]

    bounds = [(1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)] * m

    for tau, maxiter in phases:
        _CACHE_X.clear()
        args = (float(tau), n)
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _obj_x,
                x,
                jac=_jac_x,
                args=args,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(10, int(maxiter)),
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                x = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        except Exception:
            pass

        a = _a_from_x(x, n)
        sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_x = x.copy()
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_x, float(best_score)


def _optimize_full_lbfgs(a0, rng, label=""):
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY or n < 8:
        return a, _internal_score(a)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = a / a.sum()

    z = np.log(a)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _internal_score(a)
    cmax0 = float(_fft_square(a).max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax0) or cmax0 <= 0.0:
        return best_a, float(best_score)

    if n <= 8192:
        phases = [
            (cmax0 / 5.0, 35),
            (cmax0 / 20.0, 30),
            (cmax0 / 80.0, 25),
        ]
    else:
        phases = [
            (cmax0 / 5.0, 20),
            (cmax0 / 25.0, 15),
        ]

    for tau, maxiter in phases:
        _CACHE_Z.clear()
        args = (float(tau), n)
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                _obj_z,
                z,
                jac=_jac_z,
                args=args,
                method="L-BFGS",
                options={
                    "maxiter": max(10, int(maxiter)),
                    "maxls": 25,
                    "ftol": 1e-14,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            pass

        zm = float(np.max(z))
        a = np.exp(z - zm)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
        a = a / a.sum()
        sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _weights_focus(b, focus, eps=0.02):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    cmax = float(b.max())
    L = int(b.size)
    if L == 0 or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L), 0.0
    z = (cmax - b) / cmax
    if focus > 0.0:
        p = np.exp(-float(focus) * z)
    else:
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = (1.0 - eps) * (p / s) + eps / L
    return p, cmax


def _optimize_full_expg(a0, time_limit, rng, focus_start=5.0, max_focus=800.0):
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 4:
        return a, _internal_score(a)

    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = a / a.sum()

    b = None
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _internal_score(a)
    best_b = None
    no_best = 0
    max_no = 800
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 25
    focus = float(focus_start)
    eta = 0.5
    eta_min = 0.005
    eta_max = 10.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            cmax = float(b.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)  # a is normalized to sum 1
        else:
            cmax = float(b.max())
            score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_b = b.copy()
            no_best = 0
        else:
            no_best += 1

        if no_best > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            no_best = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.3)
            base = best_a.copy()
            if resets % 2 == 0:
                fresh = _random_positive(n, rng, amp=3.0)
                a = 0.78 * base + 0.22 * fresh
            else:
                a = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n))
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
            a = a / a.sum()
            b = None
            focus = float(rng.choice([2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 250.0]))
            eta = 0.5
            continue

        p, _ = _weights_focus(b, focus)
        g = 2.0 * _fft_grad(p, a)
        gd = g - float(np.dot(a, g))
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))
        if maxg < 1e-18:
            break
        gd = gd / maxg

        a_try = a * np.exp(-eta * gd)
        a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-15)
        st = float(a_try.sum())
        if st <= 1e-300:
            break
        a_try = a_try / st

        b_try = _fft_square(a_try)
        cmax_try = float(b_try.max())
        score_try = float(2.0 * n * cmax_try)
        wt_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        wt_new = float(np.dot(p, b_try))

        accept = False
        if score_try < score - 1e-13:
            accept = True
        elif cmax_try < cmax * 1.001 and wt_new < wt_old - 1e-14:
            accept = True

        if accept:
            a = a_try
            b = b_try
            eta = min(eta * 1.05, eta_max)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.72, eta_min)
            if eta < 0.02:
                a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, size=n))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
                a = a / a.sum()
                b = None
                focus = float(rng.choice([2.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 300.0]))
                eta = 0.25

        focus = min(focus * 1.012, float(max_focus))

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _polish_x(x0, n, time_limit, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 8:
        a = _a_from_x(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64), n)
        return np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), a, _internal_score(a)

    m = n // 2
    t = 1.0 / m
    eps = 1e-12
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64), eps, 1.0 - eps)
    P = _a_from_x(x, n)
    c = _fft_square(P)
    best_max = float(c.max())
    best_P = P.copy()
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_max)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_improve_sweeps = 0
    recompute_after_trials = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 >= float(time_limit):
            break

        improved = False
        order = np.random.permutation(m)

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() - t0 >= float(time_limit):
                break

            i = int(i)
            l = i
            r = n - 1 - i
            old_x = float(x[i])

            # A few well-separated targets. L-BFGS has already done smooth
            # fractional motion; this exact polish mainly improves local maxima.
            for target in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0):
                if abs(target - old_x) < 1e-9:
                    continue
                new_left = t * float(target)
                delta = new_left - P[l]
                if abs(delta) < 1e-13:
                    continue
                if new_left < -1e-10 or new_left > t + 1e-10:
                    continue

                # Apply move: P -> P + delta*(e_l - e_r).
                c[l:l + n] += 2.0 * delta * P
                c[r:r + n] -= 2.0 * delta * P
                c[2 * l] += delta * delta
                c[2 * r] += delta * delta
                c[l + r] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

                trial_max = float(c.max())
                recompute_after_trials += 1

                if trial_max < best_max - 1e-14:
                    P[l] = new_left
                    P[r] = t - new_left
                    x[i] = float(target)
                    c = _fft_square(P)
                    best_max = float(c.max())
                    best_P = P.copy()
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_max)
                    improved = True
                    break

                # Revert.
                c[r:r + n] += 2.0 * delta * P
                c[l:l + n] -= 2.0 * delta * P
                c[2 * l] -= delta * delta
                c[2 * r] -= delta * delta
                c[l + r] += 2.0 * delta * delta

                if recompute_after_trials > 4000:
                    c = _fft_square(P)
                    recompute_after_trials = 0

        if not improved:
            no_improve_sweeps += 1
            if no_improve_sweeps >= 3:
                break
            # Small random escape: try 30 random moves.
            for _ in range(30):
                if time.monotonic() - t0 >= float(time_limit):
                    break
                i = int(rng.integers(0, m))
                target = float(rng.random())
                old_x = float(x[i])
                if abs(target - old_x) < 1e-8:
                    continue
                l = i
                r = n - 1 - i
                new_left = t * target
                delta = new_left - P[l]
                if abs(delta) < 1e-13:
                    continue
                if new_left < -1e-10 or new_left > t + 1e-10:
                    continue

                c[l:l + n] += 2.0 * delta * P
                c[r:r + n] -= 2.0 * delta * P
                c[2 * l] += delta * delta
                c[2 * r] += delta * delta
                c[l + r] -= 2.0 * delta * delta

                trial_max = float(c.max())
                if trial_max < best_max - 1e-14:
                    P[l] = new_left
                    P[r] = t - new_left
                    x[i] = target
                    c = _fft_square(P)
                    best_max = float(c.max())
                    best_P = P.copy()
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_max)
                    improved = True
                    no_improve_sweeps = 0
                    break

                    # not reached: revert only if not accepted
                c[r:r + n] += 2.0 * delta * P
                c[l:l + n] -= 2.0 * delta * P
                c[2 * l] -= delta * delta
                c[2 * r] -= delta * delta
                c[l + r] += 2.0 * delta * delta
        else:
            no_improve_sweeps = 0

    # Ensure exact normalization and pair structure.
    best_x = np.clip(best_x, eps, 1.0 - eps)
    best_P = _a_from_x(best_x, n)
    best_score = _internal_score(best_P)
    return best_x, best_P, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point. Returns a list of non-negative floats minimizing the score.
    """
    seed = int(seed)
    total = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    final_deadline = start + total - 45.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng((seed * 1000003 + 12345) % (2**32))

    n_main = 8192
    m_main = n_main // 2

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    best_pair_x = None
    best_pair_score = float("inf")

    x_list = []
    x_score_list = []

    def_like_comment = None  # no nested functions are used; this line is harmless and can be ignored.

    # Keep existing construction if available.
    given = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if given is not None:
        try:
            g = np.asarray(given, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, 1000.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 0.01 and g.size >= 16:
                gn = g / s
                sc = _internal_score(gn)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = gn.copy()
                if gn.size == n_main:
                    x = _x_from_sequence(gn, n_main)
                    xa = _a_from_x(x, n_main)
                    xsc = _internal_score(xa)
                    if np.isfinite(xsc):
                        x_score_list.append(float(xsc))
                        x_list.append(x.copy())
                    if np.isfinite(xsc) and xsc < best_pair_score:
                        best_pair_score = xsc
                        best_pair_x = x.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Quick deterministic sinusoidal anti-palindromic candidates.
    idx = np.arange(m_main, dtype=np.float64)
    smid = (idx + 0.5) / float(m_main)

    def_make_sinusoid_locals = None  # no nested function; placeholder only.

    for freq in (1, 2, 3):
        for amp in (0.22, 0.38, 0.55):
            for _ in range(6):
                phase = float(rng.normal())
                x = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * smid + phase)
                x = np.clip(x, 1e-4, 1.0 - 1e-4)
                a = _a_from_x(x, n_main)
                sc = _internal_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    x_score_list.append(float(sc))
                    x_list.append(x.copy())
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = a.copy()
                    if sc < best_pair_score:
                        best_pair_score = sc
                        best_pair_x = x.copy()

    # Random binary anti-palindromic candidates.
    for _ in range(160):
        x = (rng.random(m_main) < 0.5).astype(np.float64)
        a = _a_from_x(x, n_main)
        sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            x_score_list.append(float(sc))
            x_list.append(x.copy())
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            if sc < best_pair_score:
                best_pair_score = sc
                best_pair_x = x.copy()

    # Random smooth anti-palindromic candidates.
    for _ in range(80):
        x = np.full(m_main, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 6):
            wk = 0.45 / (0.6 * k + 1.0)
            ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * smid
            x += wk * (float(rng.normal()) * np.sin(ang) + float(rng.normal()) * np.cos(ang))
        x = np.clip(x, 1e-4, 1.0 - 1e-4)
        a = _a_from_x(x, n_main)
        sc = _internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            x_score_list.append(float(sc))
            x_list.append(x.copy())
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            if sc < best_pair_score:
                best_pair_score = sc
                best_pair_x = x.copy()

    # Low-dimensional Fourier optimization.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 180.0:
        fourier_configs = [
            (4, 0.90, 4, 80),
            (6, 0.95, 4, 70),
            (8, 0.98, 3, 60),
        ]
        if _HAVE_SCIPY:
            for K, amp, restarts, maxiter in fourier_configs:
                if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline - 160.0:
                    break
                xf, scf = _optimize_fourier(n_main, K, amp, restarts, maxiter, rng)
                if xf is not None and np.isfinite(scf):
                    af = _a_from_x(xf, n_main)
                    scf = _internal_score(af)
                    if np.isfinite(scf):
                        x_score_list.append(float(scf))
                        x_list.append(xf.copy())
                        if scf < best_score:
                            best_score = scf
                            best_a = af.copy()
                        if scf < best_pair_score:
                            best_pair_score = scf
                            best_pair_x = xf.copy()

    # Select top anti-palindromic x candidates and refine with full L-BFGS.
    top_x = []
    if len(x_score_list) > 0:
        order = np.argsort(x_score_list)
        seen_score = 0.0
        for idx2 in order:
            idx2 = int(idx2)
            sc = float(x_score_list[idx2])
            if sc > seen_score + 1e-12 or len(top_x) < 3:
                seen_score = sc
                top_x.append(x_list[idx2])
            else:
                if np.array_equal(top_x[-1], x_list[idx2]):
                    continue
            if len(top_x) >= 6:
                break

    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
        for xx in top_x[:5]:
            if time.monotonic() >= final_deadline - 150.0:
                break
            try:
                aa, xa, scxx = _optimize_pair_lbfgs(xx, n_main, rng)
                if np.isfinite(scxx):
                    if scxx < best_score:
                        best_score = scxx
                        best_a = aa.copy()
                    if scxx < best_pair_score:
                        best_pair_score = scxx
                        best_pair_x = xa.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Full-parameter L-BFGS refinement on the best overall sequence.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 16384 and _HAVE_SCIPY:
        if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
            try:
                aa, sc = _optimize_full_lbfgs(best_a, rng)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = aa.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Exact anti-palindromic coordinate polish, if we have a good pair x.
    if best_pair_x is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 80.0:
        polish_time = min(360.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 50.0)
        if polish_time > 30.0:
            try:
                px, pa, psc = _polish_x(best_pair_x, n_main, polish_time, rng)
                if np.isfinite(psc):
                    if psc < best_score:
                        best_score = psc
                        best_a = pa.copy()
                    if psc < best_pair_score:
                        best_pair_score = psc
                        best_pair_x = px.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Robust projected exponentiated-gradient refinement on the best sequence.
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size <= 32768:
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
        if rem > 40.0:
            elapsed_full = time.monotonic() - (total - 120.0)
            exp_time = min(420.0, rem, max(40.0, total * 0.18))
            if exp_time > 30.0:
                try:
                    focus0 = 10.0 if best_score > 1.6 else 30.0
                    aa, sc = _optimize_full_expg(best_a, exp_time, rng, focus_start=focus0)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_a = aa.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # One final short polish if possible.
    if best_pair_x is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 30.0:
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
        if 10.0 < rem < 400.0 or rem > 400.0:
            pol_t = min(180.0, rem - 10.0)
            if pol_t > 10.0:
                try:
                    px, pa, psc = _polish_x(best_pair_x, n_main, pol_t, rng)
                    if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < best_score:
                        best_score = psc
                        best_a = pa.copy()
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = _internal_score(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s

    # Threshold tiny values and re-normalize.
    for thr in (1e-16, 1e-14, 1e-12):
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline:
            break
        a2 = best_a.copy()
        a2[a2 < thr] = 0.0
        s2 = float(a2.sum())
        if s2 > 0.01:
            a2 = a2 / s2
            sc2 = _internal_score(a2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_a = a2
                best_score = sc2

    return [float(v) for v in best_a]
```
